Title: Guida spirituale, che disinvolge l'anima, e la conduce per l'interior camino all'acquisto della perfetta contemplatione, e del ricco tesoro della pace interiore; aggiuntovi un breve Trattato della quotidiana Communione dell'istesso Autore
Creator: Miguel de Molinos (Michele di Molinos), priest
Publisher: In Venetia, per Gio. Giacomo Hertz
Publication Date: 1678
Rights: Public Domain (published 1678, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item's own rights statement reads 'http://creativecommons.org/publicdomain/mark/1.0/')
Language: ita
Source: Internet Archive item bub_gb_2rvxeLdiuHkC (https://archive.org/details/bub_gb_2rvxeLdiuHkC), the item's own bub_gb_2rvxeLdiuHkC_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: unknown library; host metadata date: 1678. Fetched directly (curl/urllib, raw bytes) by the Library thread (Era VIII pass), 2026-10-23. sha256 of the raw fetched bytes: eb4bb145aa043d4b7455398ce15ca9d393526b3d1b81a0616f9491b6d2211258.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. Google scan of a copy from the library of the Jesuit Collegio Romano (its ex-libris is on the first leaf), contributed to the Internet Archive without a named library; good OCR of old Italian with the long s as f and u for v. Repeated "Digitized by Google" lines are left in place.
Content note: The Spiritual Guide (first printed Rome, 1675) in the Venice edition of 1678 dedicated to Girolamo Gradenigo: the approbations, the author's Proemio, the three books on darkness and dryness, spiritual martyrdom and annihilation, and inward peace, and the short Treatise on daily communion.
